Arthur’s model is the main pick’em choice. The sportsbook favorite is a comparison benchmark only. The current model is unproven and historically underperformed the market favorite. It does not have a demonstrated betting edge. Prices are source observations, not verified executable offers. No wagers are placed.

Your weekly plan

  1. Wednesday: review Arthur’s model pick for Thursday, its recent-game reasoning, and whether it differs from the sportsbook favorite before your league locks.
  2. Sunday: review the remaining games again before their lock. Review updated model picks and the market comparison where your league permits changes. The favorite does not override the model. If the whole slate locks Thursday, keep those submitted picks.
  3. Before submitting: check the collection time, kickoff and current odds. Verify stale or missing prices if you use the market comparison. A missing or even market does not erase an available model pick. Injury and weather changes are not independently adjusted by this model.

This page refreshes hourly while the publishing computer is running; it does not submit league picks. Monday games follow your league’s deadline. Betting research below is for observation, not a profitable betting system.

How the model compares · Historical results

On the same 2021–2025 games, historical moneyline favorites were 900–450–4 (66.7% excluding ties); the reconstructed recent-margin model was 853–497–4 (63.2%). That is 47 fewer model winners. These are retrospective results, not a live record; historical price timing is unverified. A separate archived-Wednesday 2023 check also favored the market: 178/271 versus 161/271. A dated archive is not proof that a quote was executable.

Higher winner accuracy does not establish betting profit: favorites pay less than even money. The market-favorite benchmark record remains separate and begins at its actual first capture, with no backfilled wins.

How to read these picks and the model’s limitations

Arthur’s main pick’em choice comes from the sign of the recent-margin model’s projected score difference, not the betting favorite. It uses recent team scoring margins and home advantage; it does not yet incorporate independent injury or weather adjustments. An exact model tie picks home by fixed convention. The sportsbook comparison uses a complete, same-source moneyline pair; equal prices mean no market preference. Neither is a guaranteed winner, and model confidence is uncalibrated. Totals are evidence only.

Model spread baseline: 108 wins, 113 losses, 1 push. No betting edge is demonstrated. Bookmaker quote-update times and executable availability are unknown; retrieval time is not quote time.

Why these picks? · Evidence & changes

Stats used for Arthur’s model direction

The model uses NYG’s average scoring margin of +1.25 over 8 prior games and WAS’s -3.75 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (1.25 − -3.75) / 2 + 1.5 = +4 points for the home team. That sign selects NYG. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Why these picks? · Evidence & changes

Stats used for Arthur’s model direction

The model uses DET’s average scoring margin of +4.125 over 8 prior games and NE’s +0.875 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 0-point home-field term: (4.125 − 0.875) / 2 + 0 = +1.625 points for the home team. That sign selects DET. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Why these picks? · Evidence & changes

Stats used for Arthur’s model direction

The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Why these picks? · Evidence & changes

Stats used for Arthur’s model direction

The model uses NO’s average scoring margin of -3.875 over 8 prior games and CAR’s -2.5 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-3.875 − -2.5) / 2 + 1.5 = +0.8125 points for the home team. That sign selects NO. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Why these picks? · Evidence & changes

Stats used for Arthur’s model direction

The model uses CLE’s average scoring margin of -1.375 over 8 prior games and HOU’s +2.125 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-1.375 − 2.125) / 2 + 1.5 = -0.25 points for the home team. That sign selects HOU. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Why these picks? · Evidence & changes

Stats used for Arthur’s model direction

The model uses ATL’s average scoring margin of -3.5 over 8 prior games and KC’s +7.375 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-3.5 − 7.375) / 2 + 1.5 = -3.9375 points for the home team. That sign selects KC. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Why these picks? · Evidence & changes

Stats used for Arthur’s model direction

The model uses IND’s average scoring margin of -5.875 over 8 prior games and MIA’s -9.25 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-5.875 − -9.25) / 2 + 1.5 = +3.1875 points for the home team. That sign selects IND. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Why these picks? · Evidence & changes

Stats used for Arthur’s model direction

The model uses ARI’s average scoring margin of -11.125 over 8 prior games and LA’s +6.625 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-11.125 − 6.625) / 2 + 1.5 = -7.375 points for the home team. That sign selects LA. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Additional source evidence · not used to generate this pick

Evidence cutoff 2026-11-11T05:52:31.895434+00:00. This evidence was collected separately from the model pick and does not supply a retrospective rationale for it.

Why these picks? · Evidence & changes

Stats used for Arthur’s model direction

The model uses LV’s average scoring margin of -5.5 over 8 prior games and SEA’s +4.625 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-5.5 − 4.625) / 2 + 1.5 = -3.5625 points for the home team. That sign selects SEA. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Additional source evidence · not used to generate this pick

Evidence cutoff 2026-11-11T05:52:31.895434+00:00. This evidence was collected separately from the model pick and does not supply a retrospective rationale for it.

Reviewed official matchup facts

These are reported statuses, not estimated point effects. A starter announcement is not kickoff confirmation; an injury designation is not an inactive list.

Why these picks? · Evidence & changes

Stats used for Arthur’s model direction

The model uses DAL’s average scoring margin of +2.25 over 8 prior games and SF’s +9.875 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (2.25 − 9.875) / 2 + 1.5 = -2.3125 points for the home team. That sign selects SF. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Additional source evidence · not used to generate this pick

Evidence cutoff 2026-11-11T05:52:31.895434+00:00. This evidence was collected separately from the model pick and does not supply a retrospective rationale for it.

Why these picks? · Evidence & changes

Stats used for Arthur’s model direction

The model uses CIN’s average scoring margin of +8.125 over 8 prior games and PIT’s +4.5 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (8.125 − 4.5) / 2 + 1.5 = +3.3125 points for the home team. That sign selects CIN. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Why these picks? · Evidence & changes

Stats used for Arthur’s model direction

The model uses BAL’s average scoring margin of -8.125 over 8 prior games and LAC’s -12.25 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-8.125 − -12.25) / 2 + 1.5 = +3.5625 points for the home team. That sign selects BAL. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Saved pregame model inputs

The model uses ATL’s average scoring margin of -2.25 over 8 prior games and CIN’s +5.75 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 0-point home-field term: (-2.25 − 5.75) / 2 + 0 = -4 points for the home team. That sign selects CIN. Saved pregame model inputs

The model uses NO’s average scoring margin of -4.25 over 8 prior games and CLE’s -4.25 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-4.25 − -4.25) / 2 + 1.5 = +1.5 points for the home team. That sign selects NO. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Saved pregame model inputs

The model uses IND’s average scoring margin of -7.625 over 8 prior games and DAL’s +0.75 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-7.625 − 0.75) / 2 + 1.5 = -2.6875 points for the home team. That sign selects DAL. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Saved pregame model inputs

The model uses CAR’s average scoring margin of -2.25 over 8 prior games and DEN’s +0.875 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-2.25 − 0.875) / 2 + 1.5 = -0.0625 points for the home team. That sign selects DEN. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Saved pregame model inputs

The model uses MIA’s average scoring margin of -11.5 over 8 prior games and DET’s +3.25 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-11.5 − 3.25) / 2 + 1.5 = -5.875 points for the home team. That sign selects DET. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Saved pregame model inputs

The model uses WAS’s average scoring margin of -2 over 8 prior games and LA’s +3.25 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-2 − 3.25) / 2 + 1.5 = -1.125 points for the home team. That sign selects LA. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Saved pregame model inputs

It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-8.125 − 2.375) / 2 + 1.5 = -3.75 points for the home team. That sign selects NYG. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Saved pregame model inputs

The model uses KC’s average scoring margin of +4.25 over 8 prior games and NYJ’s -2.25 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (4.25 − -2.25) / 2 + 1.5 = +4.75 points for the home team. That sign selects KC. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

The model uses LAC’s average scoring margin of -11.125 over 8 prior games and HOU’s -2.125 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (-11.125 − -2.125) / 2 + 1.5 = -3 points for the home team. That sign selects HOU. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Saved pregame model inputs

The model uses NE’s average scoring margin of +2.75 over 8 prior games and GB’s +0.875 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (2.75 − 0.875) / 2 + 1.5 = +2.4375 points for the home team. That sign selects NE. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Saved pregame model inputs

The model uses SF’s average scoring margin of +2.875 over 8 prior games and LV’s -1.125 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (2.875 − -1.125) / 2 + 1.5 = +3.5 points for the home team. That sign selects SF. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Saved pregame model inputs

The model uses CHI’s average scoring margin of +6.75 over 8 prior games and TB’s +3.375 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (6.75 − 3.375) / 2 + 1.5 = +3.1875 points for the home team. That sign selects CHI. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.

Saved pregame model inputs

The model uses MIN’s average scoring margin of +7.5 over 8 prior games and BUF’s +6.125 over 8. It halves the difference, then adds a 1.5-point home-field term: (7.5 − 6.125) / 2 + 1.5 = +2.1875 points for the home team. That sign selects MIN. The inputs are up to eight completed games within 365 days, with a 12-hour buffer. ATS records and sportsbook prices are comparison context, not inputs to this outright-winner calculation. Opponent strength, player availability and weather are not independently adjusted here.
